iṉi, mūvakait tamiḻvaḻakkamum nutaliyatum, avaṟṟuḷ iyaṟṟamiḻē nutaliyatum, atikāram nutaliyatum, atikārattuḷ ōttu nutaliyatum, ōttiṉuṭ cūttiram nutaliyatumeṉavum ivai nutaliyataṟitaṟ pakutiyāy aṭaṅkumeṉpāṉ,
"maṉatti ṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇṭu
iṉattiṟ cērtti yuṇarttal vēṇṭum"
eṉkiṉṟāṉeṉpatu. mēlvarukiṉṟavaṟṟukkumokkum. aḵtēl. itaṉai āṇṭuvaittu maṟṟataṉai īṇṭuvaippiṉum iḵtokkum piṟaveṉiṉ, - aṟṟaṉṟu; nūliṉvēṟākiya pāyirattuḷḷāyiṉum in nutaliyataṟital varutalum uttivakaiyeṉṟaṟku itaṉai muṉvaittā ṉeṉpatu.
(2) atikāramuṟaimaieṉpatu: muṉṉiṉṟa  cūttirap poruṇmai piṉvaruñ cūttirattiṟkum peṟaṟpālaṉa peṟavaittal; avai,
"iyaṟkaip poruḷai yiṟṟeṉak kiḷattal"	(tol. col. 19)
eṉṟavaḻi iṟṟeṉak kiḷatta lurimaipūṇṭataṉṟē, ataṉaic, 
"ceyaṟkaip poruḷai yākkamoṭu kūṟal"	(tol. col. 20)
eṉpuḻiyuṅ koḷḷa vaittalum;
"kuṟṟiya likara niṟṟal vēṇṭum"	(tol. eḻut. 34)
eṉpataṉaik,
"kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntu"	(tol. eḻut. 36)
niṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟu koḷḷa vaittalum pōlvaṉa.
iṉi, vaḻakkiyalum vaḻakkiyalāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa ceyyuḷiyalum paṟṟi eḻunta ilakkaṇam iyaṟṟamiḻeṉappaṭum. acceyyuḷiṉṟi amaiyāta icaiyilakkaṇam icaittamiḻeṉappeyareyti avviyaṟ ṟamiḻp piṉṉar vaikkappaṭṭateṉappaṭum; ivviraṇṭaṉvaḻi nikaḻttuṅ kūttilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiya nāṭakat tamiḻ avaṟṟup piṉṉarttā meṉa muṟaimai kūṟutalum, iṉi iyaṟṟamiḻuḷḷum eḻuttatikārat toṭu collatikārattiṟkuñ collatikārattoṭu poruḷatikārattiṟ kum iyaipu kūṟutalum, atikārattuḷ ōttup palavākaliṉ avai oṉṟaṉpiṉoṉṟu vaittaṟku iyaipu kūṟutalum, avvāṟē cūttirat tiṟku iyaipu kūṟutalumellām atikāramuṟaimaikku iṉameṉṟu cērttiyuṇarappaṭum. piṟavum aṉṉa.
atikārameṉṟa poruṇmai yeṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, muṟaimaiyeṉa vum, iṭameṉavuṅ, kiḻamaiyeṉavuṅ koḷḷappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ, īṇṭu atikārameṉṟoḻiyātu muṟaimaiyeṉavuṅ kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, atikāra meṉpatu muṟaimaip poruṭṭaṉṟi muṉ ōriṭattu niṟuttiyataṉai vaḻimuṟaiyāṟ poruḷkōṭal koḷka; eṉṉai? muṉṉum piṉṉum niṉṟa cūttiram iraṇṭaṉaiyum ōriṭattaṉavākak karutal vēṇṭum; aṅṅaṉaṅ karutaliyaipukoṇṭaṉṟi oṉṟaṉ poruṇmai oṉṟaṟku varuvittal  aritākalāṉum, aṅṅaṉam iṭam paṟṟākkāl oruvaṉ ceyta nūloṭu piṟitorunūṟku iyaipu kūṟātavāṟupōla eḻuttatikārat toṭu collatikārattiṭai iyaipu kūṟal vēṇṭuvataṉṟāvāṉ cellumātalāṉumeṉpatu. eṉavē, iṭameṉappaṭṭatutāṉē muṟaimaip poruḷum paṭumāyiṉum iccūttirattuḷ atikārameṉavum muṟaimaiyeṉavuṅ kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ īṇṭu iṭamuṟaimai yeṉpatē karuttāyiṟṟu. itaṉāṉē uṭaṉpiṟantāruḷ oruvaṟkuriyatu vaḻit tōṉṟiṉārkkum oruvaḻi uriyavāṟupōla, muṉṉar niṉṟa viti piṉṉar vantataṟkum vēṇṭiyavaḻik koḷḷappaṭumeṉpatu uttivakaiyāyiṟṟu.
iṉiyorucārār, iṅṅaṉam varaintukoṇṭa iṭattuḷḷē yāṟṟoḻukkup pōlavaṉṟi iṭaiyiṭaiyum peṟumeṉpatu nōkki arimā nōkkun, tēraip pāyttuḷum, paruntu viḻukkāṭum āki varumeṉavuñ collupa; avaiyum iṉattiṟ cērtti yuṇarttavēpaṭu meṉpatu.
(3) tokuttuk kūṟal: tokuttiyātta nūluḷḷun tokuttuk kūṟutal;
avaiyāvaṉa: eḻuttu muppattumūṉṟu, colliraṇṭu, poruḷiraṇṭu eṉa āciriyaṉṟāṉē tokai kūṟutal pōlvaṉa.
"irutiṇai maruṅki ṉaimpā laṟiya
vīṟṟiṉiṉ ṟicaikkum patiṉō reḻuttum"	(tol. col. 10)
eṉap pala cūttirattu virintaṉa tokuttalum,
"āvayiṉ mūṉṟō ṭappati ṉaintum
pālaṟi vanta vuyartiṇaip peyar"	(tol. col. 164)
eṉa oru cūttirattu virittut tokuttalum pōlvaṉavum ataṉ pakutiyāy aṭaṅkum.
(4) vakuttu meynniṟuttal: aṅṅaṉan tokuttuk kūṟiyavaḻi eḻuttu muppattumūṉṟeṉṟa tokaiyiṉaik kuṟṟeḻuttum neṭ ṭeḻuttum uyirum uyirmeyyum valliṉamum melliṉamum iṭai yiṉamumeṉṟāṟ pōlavum, uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇai yeṉṟa tokai yiṉai oruvaṉ orutti palar oṉṟu palaveṉṟāṟpōlavum vakuttal :
"uyartiṇaik kurimaiyu maḵṟiṇaik kurimaiyu
māyiru tiṇaikku mōraṉṉa vurimaiyum"	(tol. col. 162)
eṉavum,
"iyaṟco ṟirico ṟicaiccol vaṭacoleṉ
ṟaṉaittē ceyyu ḷīṭṭac collē"	(tol. col. 387)
eṉavum varuvaṉavum avai. ivaṟṟuḷ irutiṇaiyeṉak kūṟippeyar mūṉṟu, viṉai mūṉṟeṉṟalum, iṉi akattiṇai puṟattiṇaiyeṉakkūṟi, akampuṟameṉak kōṭalum iṉameṉṟatu, avai kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭṭeḻutteṉa muṟkūṟiyaṉavē maṟṟoru peyar peṟṟāṟ pōlātu colvakaiyāṉ mūṉṟāvatoru col vēṟu peṟṟilāmaiyiṉeṉpatu. iṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷākkāl irutiṇaip peyarum viṉaiyum mummūṉṟeṉal ākātaṉṟōveṉpatu.
"colleṉap paṭupa peyarē viṉaiyeṉṟu
āyiraṇ ṭeṉpa vaṟintici ṉōrē"	(tol. col. 160)
eṉṟavaḻi,
"iṭaiccoṟ kiḷaviyu muriccoṟ kiḷaviyum
avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṟ ṟōṉṟu meṉpa"	(tol. col. 161)
eṉa vakuttalum, iṉi ārāynta iyaṟcolloṭu vēṟu mūṉṟu coṟkūṭṭi nāṉkeṉṟalum avvāṟē iṉameṉappaṭum. eṉṉai? oḻinta mūṉṟu collum iyaṟcolliṉ vēṟupaṭṭamai kūṟumākali ṉeṉpatu. aḵtēl, eḻuttu muppattumūṉṟeṉak kūṟip piṉṉar irunūṟṟorupattāṟuyir meyk kuppai kōṭalum vēṟṟeḻuttāki varumeṉpatu 'vakuttu meynniṟutta'leṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷāmō veṉiṉ, kōṭumaṉṟē,
"puḷḷi yillā vellā meyyum"	(tol. eḻut. 17)
eṉpuḻi,
"uruvuru vāki yakaramo ṭuyirttalum"
eṉa āciriyaṉ iraṇṭeḻuttiṉ kūṭṭamākac cūttirañ ceyti lāṉāyiṉeṉpatu.
'mey'yeṉṟataṉāṉ vakuttavaṟṟaip piṉṉum vakuttu niṟuttal koḷḷappaṭum; eṉṉai? iyaṟcīr patteṉavum āciriyavuriccīrā ṟeṉavum vakuttavaṟṟai,
"iyaṟcī riṟutimuṉ nērava ṇiṟpiṉ
uriccīr veṇpā vāku meṉpa"	(tol. cey. 19)
eṉa iyalacaimayakkamākiya nāṉkaṉaiyē iyaṟcīreṉavum,
"veṇpā vuriccī rāciriya vuriccīr
iṉpā nēraṭik koruṅkunilai yilavē"	(tol. cey. 23)
eṉṟavaḻi, āciriyavuriccīr āṟaṉuḷ iraṇṭaṉai oḻittu uriyacai mayakkamākiya nāṉkaṉaiyē āciriyavuriccīreṉavum vakuttu niṉṟamaiyi ṉeṉpatu.
'mey'yeṉṟataṉāṉ vakuttavaṟṟaip piṉṉum vakuttu niṟuttal koḷḷappaṭum; eṉṉai?
"iyalacai mayakka miyaṟcī rēṉai
uriyacai mayakka māciriya vuriccīr"	(tol. cey. 13)
eṉṟu pakuttōtiyavāṟṟāṉeṉpatu; akkaruttiṉāṉaṉṟē oḻinta iraṇṭaṉaiyuṅ,
"kalittaḷai maruṅkiṟ kaṭiyavum paṭāa"	(tol. cey. 24)
eṉa iṟantatutaḻīi uraippāṉāyiṟṟeṉpatu.
(5) moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa vavvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉai muṭṭiṉṟu: muṭittaleṭuttōtiya poruṇmaikku ēṟṟa vakaiyāṉ apporuṇmaikkaṭ collātatoṉṟu koḷḷavaittal.
'muṭṭiṉṟi muṭitta'leṉṟataṉāṉ eṭuttōtātatum eṭut tōtiyataṉōṭu okkuñ ciṟappiṟṟeṉṟavāṟām. atu pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. maṟṟu itaṉai aruttāpatti yeṉṉāmō veṉiṉ, eṉṉā maṉṟē,
"piṟacī ruḷvaḻit taṉṟaḷai vēṇṭupa"
eṉṉum poruṭṭaṉṟit taṉcīroṭu iyaṟcīr vantu taḷai koḷḷumeṉa moḻiyātoru poruḷkōṭaliṉeṉka.
iṉi,
"raḵkā ṉoṟṟum pakara viṟutiyum"	(tol. col. 7)
eṉṟavaḻi oḻinta nāṉkeḻuttum īṟṟiṉiṟkumeṉṟu kōṭal pōl vaṉavum ataṉ iṉameṉappaṭum. eṉṉai? oṉṟeṉamuṭittal taṉṉiṉa muṭitta'leṉiṉum iḻukkātu. iṉic,
"cīriyai maruṅki ṉōracai yoppiṉ"	(tol. cey. 56)
eṉṟavaḻi, oṉṟātatu iyaṟcīrveṇṭaḷai yeṉṟukōṭal aruttāpatti yāki,
"eṭutta moḻiyiṉañ ceppalu murittu"	(tol. cey. 61)
eṉṟa vaḻakkiyalāṉē vaḻuvaṉṟi aṭaṅkumeṉpatu.
(6) vārātataṉāṉ vantatu muṭittal:oru poruṇmaikku vēṇṭum ilakkaṇam nirampa vārātatoru cūttirattāṉē aṅṅaṉam vanta poruṇmaikku vēṇṭum muṭipu koḷḷacceytal; atu,
"toṭara liṟuti tammuṟ ṟāmvariṉ
lakaram ṟakaravoṟ ṟākalu murittē"	(tol. eḻut. 214)
eṉa akarattiṟkuk kēṭu vārāta cūttirattāṉē lakaram ṟakaravoṟṟāy varumeṉap pirittut tiripu kūṟiyatē paṟṟāka akaram āṇṭu nillātu keṭumeṉṟu koḷḷavaittu ataṉāṉē 'ciṟcila vittip paṟpala koṇṭā'reṉṟu vanta puṇarcci muṭittavāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.
"ellā meṉṉu miṟuti muṉṉar
vaṟṟeṉ cāriyai muṟṟat tōṉṟum"	(tol. eḻut. 189)
eṉṟavaḻi, ellāmeṉṉum viravuppeyaruḷ uyartiṇai kūṟiṉ vaṟṟuccāriyai peṟāmaiyāṉ atu peṟṟuvanta aḵṟiṇaik kūṟē muṭittalum atu; piṟavum aṉṉa.
iṉi, iṅṅaṉam muṭipukōṭalaṉṟi, āṇṭuk kuṟiyiṭutalum, āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum oruṅkunikaḻntatu piṉṉarāṭcikkaṇ vārāmaiyum vantavaḻip piṟavaṟṟoṭu kūṟutalum ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. avai,
"avvakai yoṉpatum viṉaiyeñcu kiḷavi"	(tol. col. 230)
eṉa muṭintataṉai mīṇṭum,
"neṟippaṭat tōṉṟu meñcuporuṭ kiḷavi"	(tol. col. 430)
eṉak kuṟiyiṭutal eccaviyaluḷ vantatāyiṉum muṉṉar akkuṟiyīṭu vantatiṉmaiyiṉ atu vārātateṉappaṭumākaliṉ aḵtu īṇṭuk kuṟiyiṭuta lāyiṟṟu. viṉaiyiyaluṭ peyareccameṉṟu āḷutalum atu.
'vaṇṇacciṉaiccol' 'muṟṟuccol' eṉpaṉavuṅ kuṟṟiya likarattaip puḷḷiyeṉṟalum āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum oruṅku nikaḻntaṉa vākaliṉ avaiyum iṉi vārāmaiyāṉ vantuḻi vantuḻi avvāṟu āṇṭāṉeṉpatu.
iṉip, puḷḷiyeṉamēl āḷa vārātataṉaip puḷḷiyeṉṟu āḷvaṉavaṟṟoṭu mayakkaṅ kūṟutaleṉpatu,
"avaitāṅ,
kuṟṟiya likaraṅ kuṟṟiya lukara
māyta meṉṟa
muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meḻuttō raṉṉa"	(tol. eḻut. 2)
eṉpuḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram puḷḷiyeṉṟu yāṇṭum āḷa vārāmaiyāṉum atutāṉ avvaḻi varavēṇṭutalāṉum aṅṅaṉam  puḷḷiyeṉṟu āḷavaruṅ kuṟṟukarattōṭum āytattōṭum uṭaṉkūṟutalāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam uṭaṉkūṟākkāṟ 'puḷḷiyuṅ kuṟṟikaramu'meṉac cūttiram peṟutal vēṇṭuvatāvāṉ cellumeṉpatu. iṉi,
"ā ē ō ammūṉṟum viṉāa"	(tol. eḻut. 32)
eṉavum,
"māṟuko ḷeccamum viṉāvu maiyamum"	(tol. eḻut. 290)
eṉavuṅ kūṟuvaṉavum avai; eṉṉai? iṭaiccollōttiṉuḷ viṉāveṉ ṟōtāta ākāram vārātatuṭaṉ āṇṭu viṉāveṉṟu ōtiya ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ,
"ā ē ō ammūṉṟum viṉāa"
eṉṟu uṭaṉ kūṟiṉamaiyāṉum, avvāṟu iṭaiccollōttiṉuḷ eṭuttōtāta māṟukoḷeccattōṭum aiyattōṭum āṇṭōtiya viṉāviṉaiyum eṇṇiṉaiyum eḻuttōttiṉuḷ vitantuṭaṉ kūṟiṉamaiyāṉumeṉpatu. itu nōkkip pōlum,
"īṟṟacai yivvain tēkāram"	(tol. col. 259)
eṉa iṭaiccolliṉai eḻuttuccāriyai peytōtiya karuttāṉē ipporuṇmaikoḷḷa vaippāṉāyiṟṟumeṉpatu.
maṟṟu ivai etiratu pōṟṟalākāvōveṉiṉ,atu poruṭ paṭaik kaṇṇateṉavum, ivai āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum paṟṟiyatoru pakuti yeṉavuṅ kūṟi viṭukka. aḵtēṟ, kuṟiyīṭṭāṉ īṇṭārāyāṉōveṉiṉ, -  ivai uttivakaiyākalāṉum, atutāṉē uttiyeṉap paṭumātalāṉum, ataṉai īṇṭu ārāyāṉeṉpatu.
(7) vantatukoṇṭu vārātatuṇarttal: piṉṉoruvaḻi vantatu koṇṭu muṉvārātatoru poruḷ aṟiyavaittal; atu,
"ñāṅkark kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikum"	(tol. eḻut. 204)
eṉpatu.
"eñciya mūṉṟum mēlvantu muṭikku
meñcuporuṭ kiḷavi yilaveṉa moḻipa"	(tol. col. 439)
eṉa, vantatu koṇṭu mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa ēḻeccattiṟkum mēl vantu muṭikkuñ col vārātataṉai varumeṉṟuṇarntu koḷḷavaittamai yiṉ ituvumatuvēyāyiṟṟu.
"āyiru tiṇaiyi ṉicaikkumaṉa collē"	(tol. col. 1)
eṉṟavaḻit tiṇaiyeṉṉum peyar epporuṭkum eytuvittal oru cūttirattuḷḷē kōṭaliṉ ataṉai ataṟku iṉameṉṟu koḷḷap paṭum; piṟavum aṉṉa.
(8) muntu moḻintataṉ ṟalaitaṭumāṟṟu:muṉṉorukāṟ kūṟiya muṟaiyaṉṟip piṉṉorukāl talai taṭumāṟākak kūṟutal; atu,
"paṉṉī ruyiru moḻimuta lākum"	(tol. eḻut. 49)
eṉavum,
"uyirmey yallaṉa moḻimuta lākā"	(tol. eḻut. 50)
eṉavum, uyirum meyyum niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉaṉṟik,
"katana pamaveṉu māvain teḻuttu
mellāvuyiroṭuñ cellumār mutalē"	(tol. eḻut. 51)
eṉavum meypaṟṟi varaiyaṟai kūṟutalum,.
"ellā moḻikku miṟutiyu mutalu
meyyē yuyireṉ ṟāyī riyala"	(tol. eḻut. 103)
eṉa muṟkūṟiya muṟaipiṟaḻak kūṟutalumāyiṉa.
meyyum uyirum paṟṟi vitantu varaiyaṟuppiṉum aḵtu iraṇṭaṟ kuñ cellumeṉṟu kōṭaṟkum, iṉi iyalpuvakaiyāṉ īṟātal orutalaiyāka uṭaiyaṉa meyyeṉṟaṟkum avvāṟu kūṟiṉāṉeṉ ṉāmōveṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉamē akkaruttiṉāṉaṉṟē 'ottakāṭci'yeṉak kuṟṟattōṭu oppumaikūṟi maṟṟup poruḷ payattaliṉ amaiyumeṉṟu koḷvāmāyiṟṟeṉpatu.
iṉi,
"mūṉṟutalai yiṭṭa muppatiṟ ṟeḻuttiṉ."	(tol. eḻut. 103)
eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa iṉameṉappaṭum.
"puḷḷi yillā vellā meyyum.;'	(tol. eḻut. 17)
eṉpatu mey muṟkūṟiṉamaiyiṉ ituvum itaṟku utāraṇameṉappaṭum.
maṟṟu, mayaṅkakkūṟalōṭu itaṉiṭai vēṟṟumai yeṉṉai yeṉiṉ, - aḵtu,
" iṉṉē vaṟṟē,"	(tol. eḻut. puṇa. 17)
eṉa niṟuttamuṟaiyāṟ kūṟātu maṟṟatuvē paṟṟāka maṟṟoru poruḷ koḷḷap paṭum. iḵtu aṉṉataṉṟi aṅṅaṉam mayaṅkakkūṟalvēṇṭum poruṇmaittāki varumeṉpatu; aḵtēl, itu māṟukoḷakkūṟa leṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamākātō veṉiṉ, ākātu; eṉṉai? itu muṟkūṟiya poruḷiṉai māṟupaṭāmaiyāṉum niṟuttamuṟai talaitaṭumāṟa vaikkun tuṇaiyākalāṉumeṉpatu.
(9) oppakkūṟal :oṉṟu kūṟuṅkāl iruporuṭkuṟittateṉṟu iraṭṭuṟacceytal. atu,
"iṉṉi ṉikara māvi ṉiṟuti
muṉṉark keṭuta lurittu mākum."	(tol. eḻut. 120)
eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa.
"viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyu muvamak kiḷaviyum"	(tol. eḻut. 204)
eṉavum,
" aṉṉa veṉṉu muvamak kiḷaviyum."	(tol. eḻut. 204)
eṉavum iṉamallaṉavaṟṟai uṭaṉeṇṇutalum,
" māmarak kiḷaviyu māvu māvum."	(tol. eḻut. 231)
eṉa māṭṭeṟiyuṅkāl vēṟuvēṟu vitiyuṭaiyaṉavaṟṟai oruṅku māṭṭeṟitalum, āṇṭu nāṉku valleḻuttiṉaiyum uṭaṉkōṭalum, nilaimoḻittoḻiloṭu varumoḻit toḻilum oppakkoṇṭu māṭṭeṟitalum pōlvaṉa oppakkūṟaleṉpataṉ pakutiyāy aṭaṅkumeṉpatu.
(10) orutalai moḻital: ōr atikārattiṟ collaṟpālataṉai vēṟu atikārattuc colli avvilakkaṇamē āṇṭuṅ koḷḷa vaittal ; atu,
" a i uam mūṉṟum cuṭṭu."	(tol. eḻut. 31)
eṉavum,
" āṇum peṇṇu. maḵṟiṇai yiyaṟkai."	(tol. eḻut. 303)
eṉavum ivai eḻuttatikārattuk kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉē collatikārat tuḷḷum avvilakkaṇaṅ koḷḷa vaittamaiyiṉ appeyarttāyiṟṟu.
uṭampoṭupuṇarttuc colluvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. eṉṉai? vitiyallātatu vitipōla maṟṟoruvaḻic cēṟaliṉ.
(11) taṉkōṭkūṟal:collātaṉa piṟavuḷavāyiṉum annūṟku vēṇṭuvatē koḷvaleṉṟal; atu.
" aḵtiva ṇuvalā teḻuntupuṟat ticaikku
meyteri vaḷiyicai yaḷavunuvaṉ ṟiciṉē."	(tol. eḻut. 102)
eṉavum,
" cakarak kiḷaviyu mavaṟṟō raṟṟē
aai auveṉu mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē."	(tol. eḻut. 61)
eṉavum,
" kuṟṟeḻut taintu moḻiniṟai pilavē."	(tol. eḻut. 44)
eṉavum,
" pāṭaluṭ payiṉṟavai nāṭuṅ kālai."	(tol. akat.  3)
eṉavum varum.
ivvāṟṟāṉē,
" aḷapiṟ kōṭa lantaṇar maṟaittu."	(tol. eḻut. 102)
āyiṉum atu kūṟilēṉeṉavum, iyaṟcolliṟkallatu nilaimoḻi yākkaṅ kūṟēṉeṉavum,
" vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum ārāyval"
eṉṟu pukuntāṉ pāṭaluṭ payiṉṟa vaḻakkē kūṟavaleṉavuṅ kūṟutaliṉ avai taṉkōṭkūṟutalām.
iṉi, oṉpatumayakkattuṇ meymmayakkaṅkūṟi oḻintaṉa kūṟāmaiyum, viṉaittokaiyum paṇputtokaiyum eṭuttōti muṭiyāmaiyum,
" nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉu mulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum
pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam."	(tol. akat. 53)
paṟṟip poruḷilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvaleṉṟalum, perumpāṉmaiya ilakkaṇa vaḻakkeṉpa eṉṟalum, ataṉāṉē ciṟupāṉmaiya mayakkameṉṟalum, am ām em ēm eṉpaṉa mutalāyavaṟṟai aṅṅaṉam pakuttōtutaṟ payaṉum, avai viṉaiyiṉṟi avviṉaiceytāṉmēl nikaḻkiṉṟa kūṟātalē paṟṟi,
" viṉaiceyaṉ maruṅkiṟ kālamoṭu varunavum."	(tol. col. 252)
eṉṟu iṭaiccollōṭu ōtutalum pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉap paṭum; eṉṉai? ivai tāṉ kūṟuvaleṉṟu pukuntavaṟṟuḷḷum oru poruḷāṉavaṟṟai varaintukoṇṭamaiyiṉ avaṟṟu iṉameṉap paṭṭaṉa.
(12) muṟaipiṟaḻāmai:kāraṇamiṉṟit tāṉ cila poruḷ eṇṇi niṟuttiyapiṉṉar ammuṟai piṟaḻntāluṅ kuṟṟamilvaḻiyum am muṟaiyiṉaiyē ilakkaṇamākac collutal ;atu,
" peyar ai oṭu ku
iṉ atu kaṇviḷi yeṉṉu mīṟṟa."	(tol. col. vēṟ. 3)
eṉa niṟutta muṟai paṟṟi eḻuvāyvēṟṟumai iraṇṭāvatu mūṉṟāva teṉap peyarkoṭuttal.
" akaramuta ṉakara viṟuvāy."	(tol. eḻut. 1)
eṉavuṅ,
" kaikkiḷai mutalāp peruntiṇai yiṟuvāy"	(tol. akat. 1)
eṉavum, vaḻakkiyalāṉum ilakkaṇavakaiyāṉum uḷporuḷai vitantē eṇṇi niṟuttātavaḻiyum,
"avaṟṟuḷ,
a i u e o"
eṉavum,
" avaṟṟuḷ,
naṭuva ṇaintiṇai naṭuvaṇa toḻiya."	(tol. akat. 2)
eṉavum muṟaipiṟaḻāmaṟkōṭal ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
(13) piṟaṉuṭampaṭṭatu tāṉuṭampaṭutal:uḷporuḷ aṉṟāyi ṉum vaḻakkiyalāṟ koḷ poruḷ ituveṉak kūṟutal ; atu,
" paṇṭiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ marīiya marapu " 	(tol. col. 91)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavaṟṟāṉ aṟika.
iṉi,
" mīyeṉa marīiya viṭamvarai kiḷaviyum"	(tol. eḻut. 250)
eṉpuḻi mēleṉpatu ilakkaṇameṉṟu eṭuttōtiyataṉai uṭkoṇṭu mīeṉṟataṉai marūuveṉṟamaiyiṉ aḵtu iṉam eṉappaṭum; eṉṉai? mutaṉūluṭ koṇṭavāṟaṟintu maṟṟu ataṉait tāṉ iccol iṉṉavāṟāyiṟṟeṉṟu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟātu uṭampaṭutaliṉ. maṟṟu ataṉai iṉameṉpa teṟṟukku? itutāṉē piṟaṉuṭampaṭṭatu tāṉuṭampaṭṭatākātō, mutaṉūlāciriyaṉ uṭampaṭṭa tākaliṉeṉiṉ, - aṟṟaṉṟu; mutaṉūlāciriyaṉaip piṟaṉeṉṉāmaiyāṉum, mutaṉūliṉ vaḻittākiya nūluḷ avaṉ uṭampaṭṭatoṉṟu uṭampaṭuvaleṉṟu uttivakaiyāṟ koḷḷātu muḻuvatūuṅ koḷvaṉākalāṉum avvāypāṭu kūṟalākāteṉpatu.
iṉi, mutaṉūluḷmēleṉpatu mīyeṉa marīiyiṟṟeṉṟu vitantōtappaṭṭataṉai aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟātu mīyeṉa marīiyiṟṟeṉṟu vāḷātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, ataṉai iṉameṉṟu koṇṭāmeṉpatu. eṉavē,  īṇṭup piṟaṉeṉṟatu vaḻakkiṉuḷḷōrai nōkkiyāyiṟṟu; eṉṟāṟku, oḻinta vaḻinūlāciriyaraip piṟaṉeṉṟāṉeṉṉāmō veṉiṉ, avaruṭampaṭṭatu uṭampaṭṭataṉāṟ payantateṉṉai? mutaṉūliṟ piṟaḻāmai nūlceyyumāyiṉeṉa maṟukka.
allatūum icainūluṅ kūttanūlum paṟṟip piṟaṉkōṭkūṟa leṉpataṉāṟ piṟaṉeṉṉiṉaṉṟi, iyaṟṟamiḻk kaṇṇē mutaṉūlāciri yaṉaip piṟaṉeṉṉāṉeṉpatu. aḵtēl, vaḻakku nūl ceyvāṉ avvaḻakkiṉai vaḻaṅkuvāraip piṟaṉeṉṉumō veṉiṉ,-ilakkaṇa mum vaḻakkumeṉa iraṇṭaṉuḷ iḵtilakkaṇamātaliṉ avvaḻakkiṉuḷ vaḻaṅkuvāraip piṟaṉeṉṟal amaiyumaṉṟōveṉpatu; eṉṟāṟku, avaruṭam paṭṭatu uṭampaṭutal uttivakaiyeṉpa teṟṟukku? aḵtu iyalpēyaṉṟō veṉiṉ. aṅṅaṉam marīiyiṉum ilakkaṇameṉpatu tiripillātākaliṟ, ṟiripupaṭum vaḻakkiṉai uṭampaṭutal ilakkaṇamaṉ ṟāyiṉum, atu vaḻakkiṉuḷḷār vēṇṭumāṟṟāṉ ilakkaṇamēyā meṉpatu karuttu ; allākkāl, eḷḷēpōla eṭkuppaiyun taṉtaṉmaiyāṉ uḷporuḷākalum vēṇṭumaṉṟōveṉpatu.
(14) iṟantatu kāttaleṉpatu: muṟkūṟiyavoru cūttirapporuṇ maiyaip piṉṉoru cūttirattāṉ vilakkutal ; atu,
" pālkeḻu kiḷavi nālvarkku murittē"	(tol. poru. 5)
eṉak kūṟiya piṉṉar.
" naṭpi ṉaṭakkai yāṅkalaṅ kaṭaiyē"	(tol. poru. 6)
eṉṟāṟpōla vilakkutal.
nūṟpuṟaṉaṭaiyum ottuppuṟaṉaṭaiyum atikārappuṟaṉaṭaiyum  pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. avai,
" īṟiyaṉ maruṅki ṉivaiyivaṟ ṟiyalpeṉak
kūṟiya kiḷavip pallā ṟellām
meyttalaip paṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi
ottavai yuriya puṇarmoḻi nilaiyē"	(tol. eḻut. 171)
eṉavum,
" puḷḷi yiṟutiyu muyiriṟu kiḷaviyuñ
colliya valla vēṉaiya vellām"	(tol. eḻut. 202)
eṉavum varuvaṉa pōlvaṉa.
(15) etiratupōṟṟal:varukiṉṟa cūttirap poruṇmaikkēṟpa vēṟoruporuḷ muṟkūṟutal ; atu,
" īṟāku puḷḷi akaramoṭu nilaiyum"	(tol. eḻut. 161)
eṉa varukiṉṟataṉai nōkki,
" āṟa ṉurupi ṉakarak kiḷavi
yīṟā kakaramuṉaik keṭutal vēṇṭum"	(tol. eḻut. 115)
eṉak kūṟutalum,
" pallōr paṭarkkai muṉṉilai taṉmai"	(tol. col. 222)
eṉa varukiṉṟataṉai nōkkit,
"taṉmaic collē yaḵṟiṇaik kiḷaviyeṉṟu
eṇṇuvaḻi maruṅkiṉ viravutal varaiyār"	(tol. col. 43)
eṉa vaḻuvamaittalum pōlvaṉa.
iṉi, oru cūttirattuḷḷē,
" īṟāku puḷḷi yakaramoṭu nilaiyum "	(tol. eḻut. 161)
eṉa varuvataṉai nōkkik kūṟiya,
" kuṟṟoṟ ṟiraṭṭa lillai	(tol. eḻut. tokai. 19)
eṉṟal ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
'(16) moḻivāmeṉṟal:orupayaṉōkki muṟkūṟuva leṉṟal; avai,
" uṇarak kūṟiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum"	(tol. eḻut. 35)
eṉavum,
" kaṭappā ṭaṟinta puṇariya lāṉa"	(tol. eḻut. 37)
eṉavum,
"avvē,
ivveṉa vaṟitaṟku meypeṟak kiḷappa"	(tol. col. 121)
eṉavum varum.
iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiyataṉāṟ payaṉ : kuṟṟukaravīṟṟukkaṇaṉṟip puṇar moḻik kuṟṟikara miṉṟeṉpatūum, iṉip puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟikaram peruvaraviṟṟeṉpatūum aṟivittalāyiṟṟu.
" avvē,
ivveṉa vaṟitaṟku meyppeṟak kiḷappa"	(tol. col. 121)
eṉpatūum,
'tām viḷi koḷḷā' (co. 126) eṉṟatūum avviḷiyilakkaṇa meṉṟaṟ payam paṭa vantaṉa; īṟṟup potuviṉāṉ viḷiyēṟkumeṉṟu ōtappaṭṭa peyaruḷ ivai viḷiyēlāveṉa viḷivilakkal vēṇṭuta lāṉum, nī vārāyeṉpatu iyalpuviḷiyaṉṟeṉa vilakkutalum viḷiyilakkaṇamē yātalāṉu meṉpatu.
iṉi,
" meyppeṟak kiḷantu poruḷvarain ticaikkum
aikāra vēṟṟumait tiripeṉa moḻipa"	(tol. eḻut. 157)
eṉavum,
" melleḻut tiyaṟkai colliya muṟaiyāṉ
ṅañanama veṉṉu moṟṟā kummē"	(tol. eḻut. 143)
eṉavum varuvaṉa iṉameṉappaṭum. eṉṉai? avaiyum muṉṉarp pōy moḻivaṉavaṟṟai avāvi niṉṟamaiyi ṉeṉpatu.
(17) kūṟiṟṟeṉṟal:muṟkūṟiyatōr ilakkaṇattiṉai maṟṟoru poruṭkum vitikkavēṇṭiyavaḻi, avvilakkaṇattiṉai mīṭṭuṅkūṟātu mēṟkūṟiyavāṟṟāṉē koḷkaveṉpāṉ avai kūṟiṉēṉeṉṟu nekiḻntu pōtal; avai,
" kaikkiḷai mutalā veḻuperun tiṇaiyu
muṟkiḷan taṉavē muṟaineṟi vakaiyiṉ"	(tol. cey. 185)
eṉavum,
"eṇvakai yiyaṉeṟi piḻaiyā tāki
muṉṉuṟak kiḷanta muṭiviṉa tatuvē"	(tol. cey. 205)
eṉavum varum. ivai akapporuṭkum puṟapporuṭkum ōtiya ilakkaṇañ ceyyuḷuḷḷum avvāṟeytuvittavāṟāyiṟṟu.
"mutaleṉap paṭuva tāyiru vakaittē"	(tol. akat. 17)
eṉavum,
"meypeṟu vakaiyē kaikōḷ vakaiyē"	(tol. cey. 188)
eṉavum,
"māttirai yaḷavu meḻuttiyal vakaiyu
mēṟkiḷan taṉṉa veṉmaṉār pulavar"	(tol. cey. 2)
eṉavum varuvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum ; eṉṉai? kaikkiḷai peruntiṇaikkum ivaiyē mutaleṉṟalāṉun, tiṇaiyuṅ kaikōḷum pōlvaṉa puṟapporuṭkuṅ kōṭaṟpayaṉpaṭa vaittamaiyāṉum, muṟkūṟiya māttiraiyum eḻuttum piṟavāṟṟāṟ ceyyuṭkup payaṉpaṭu māṟṟāṉ vēṟupaṭṭavallatu avai mēṟkūṟiya māttiraiyum eḻuttumē eṉṟamaiyāṉumeṉpatu.
(18) tāṉ kuṟiyiṭutal:ulakukuṟiyiṉṟit taṉṉūluḷḷē vēṟu kuṟiyiṭṭāḷal ; avai,
uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇaiyeṉavum, kaikkiḷai peruntiṇai yeṉavum, colliṟkum poruḷiṟkum vaḻakkiyalāṉaṉṟi āciriyaṉ tāṉē kuṟiyiṭutal. vaṇṇac ciṉaiccoṉ, muṟṟuviṉaiccol leṉa āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum oruṅku nikaḻntaṉavum, viṉaiyeñcukiḷaviyum peyareñcukiḷaviyumeṉṟu āṇṭu,
"... āyī raintum
neṟippaṭat tōṉṟu meñcuporuṭ kiḷavi"	(tol. col. 430)
eṉak kuṟiyiṭutalum atu.
"iraṇṭākuvatē
aiyeṉap peyariya vēṟṟumaik kiḷavi"	(tol. col. 72)
eṉavum,
" mūṉṟaṉu maintaṉun tōṉṟak kūṟiya
ākkamoṭu puṇarnta vētuk kiḷavi"	(tol. col. 93)
eṉavum peyar koṭuttal ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
(19) orutalaiyaṉmai: uṟuti allāta nilai.
"vēṟṟumai yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikum"	(tol. eḻut. 225)
eṉa virittāṟ pōlātu, ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiyoḻital :
atu,
" kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāytap puḷḷi
yuyiroṭu puṇarntaval lāṟaṉ micaittē"	(tol. eḻut. 38)
eṉṟakkāl yāṇṭum orutalaiyāka vārātu, varuñāṉṟu varuvatu āṇṭeṉṟu koḷḷavaittal.
iṉic, collōttiṉuḷ vēṟṟumaiyeṉṟu ōtappaṭṭa eṭṭaṉuḷ eḻuvāy vēṟṟumaiyiṉaiyum viḷivēṟṟumaiyiṉaiyum 'vēṟṟumai'yeṉṉātu eḻuttōttiṉuḷ 'alvaḻi'yeṉṟal pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
(20) muṭintatu kāṭṭal:collukiṉṟa poruṭku vēṇṭuvaṉa vellāñ collātu tollāciriyar kūṟiṉāreṉṟu collutal.
aḵtu 'oṉṟaṟivatu uṟṟaṟiva'teṉṟaṟkum, ' iraṇṭaṟivatu uṟṟuñ cuvaittum aṟivatu' eṉṟaṟkum muṟaimaiyāṟ cūttirañ ceyvāṉ avai avvāṟātaṟkuk kāraṇaṅ kūṟātu, 
" nēriti ṉuṇarntōr neṟippaṭut tiṉarē"	(tol. mara. 27)
eṉa muṭintatu kāṭṭal.
' nuṇṇiti ṉuṇarntōr kaṇṭa vāṟē'	(tol. eḻut. 7)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
(21) āṇai kūṟal:ivvāciriyaṉ karuttu ituveṉak koḷḷa vaittal : atu,
" ammi ṉiṟuti kacatak kālait
taṉmey tirintu ṅañana vākum"	(tol. eḻut. 129)
eṉak karuviyōttiṉuṭ cāriyai makaram pakara varumoḻikkaṇ tiriyā teṉṟu pōyc ceykaiyuḷ valleḻuttiṉatu iyaṟkai melleḻut tātal aṟivittaṟku 'alvaḻi yellā melleḻuttāku'meṉac collutal pōlvaṉa. ṅañana ātaṟkum iḵtokkum.
" ompaṭai yāṇaiyiṟ kiḷantavaṟ ṟiyalāṟ
pāṅkuṟa vuṇarta leṉmaṉār pulavar"	(tol. col. 396)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
maṟṟut taṉkōṭkūṟalōṭu itaṉiṭai vēṟṟumai yeṉṉai yeṉiṉ, atu tantirañ ceyyum pakutikkaṇṇatu ; iḵtu aṉṉataṉṟip puṇarccikkaṭ ciṟappuṭaiya nilaimoḻi varumoḻit tiripu pōlā teṉṟu karuviyākiya iṭaiccoṟkāyiṉ ittuṇai yamaiyumeṉṟu āṇai ceytaliṉ appeyarttāyiṟṟu.
(22) palporuṭkēṟpiṉ nallatu kōṭaloru cūttirattuṭ payanta coṟṟoṭar palaporuṭ kēṟṟatāyiṉum nallatu koḷkiṉṟāreṉak karuti avvāṟu ceytal : avai,
" oruva reṉṉum peyarnilaik kiḷavi
yirupāṟku murittē teriyuṅ kālai"	(tol. col. 193)
eṉṟavaḻi, oruvareṉpatoru col taṉkaṇṇē irupālāraiyun taḻīi niṟkumeṉavum, atu karuviyāka irupālāraiyuñ collappaṭumeṉavum kavarttavaḻi,
" taṉmai cuṭṭiṟ paṉmaik kēṟkum"	(tol. col. 194)
eṉpataṉoṭu paṭuttunōkka irupālāraiyum orucol taṉkaṭ ṭaḻīi niṟṟalē nallateṉṟu koḷḷavaittal.
" numme ṉiṟuti yiyaṟkai yākum"	(tol. eḻut. 187)
eṉpuḻi, eḻuttu vikāramuṭaiyataṉaik kaḷaintu cāriyaikkaṇṇē iyaṟkai kōṭal ataṉpāṟpaṭum. itaṉai ēṟpuḻik kōṭaleṉavum orupuṭaic cēṟaleṉavuñ collupa.
(23) tokuttamoḻiyāṉ vakuttaṉar kōṭal:oru vāypāṭu eṭuttōtap palavāypāṭu ataṟku vantu pūṇumeṉṟu vakuttuk koḷḷavaittal ; atu,
"ceytu ceyyūc ceypu ceyteṉac
ceyyiyar ceyyiya ceyiṉceyac ceyaṟkeṉa
avvakai yoṉpatum viṉaiyeñcu kiḷavi"	(tol. col. 230)
eṉavum,
"kāppi ṉoppi ṉūrtiyi ṉiḻaiyiṉ"	(tol. col. 73)
eṉavum oru vāypāṭu tokuttu ōtiyavāṟṟāṉē pala vāypāṭu vakuttuk koḷḷavaippateṉpatu, ceyteṉpataṉai nakku vantu kaṇṭu niṉṟu pāṭip pōy eṉap palavākkutalum cey eṉṉum mutaṉilaiyāṉ muṟṟuvāypāṭu palavumākkutalum, iṉik 'kāppi' ṉeṉṟavaḻip
"purattal puṟantara lōmputal pōṟṟal"
eṉap palavāka vakuttaluṅ kaṇṭukoḷka.
" uruveṉa moḻiyiṉum"	(tol. col. 24)
eṉṟalum,
" itaṉa tituviṟṟu"	(tol. col. 111)
eṉṟalum pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
(24) maṟutalaicitaittut taṉṟuṇipuraittal:oru poruḷiṉai oruvaṉ vēṟupaṭakkoḷvatōr uṇarvutōṉṟiyakkāl av vēṟupāṭṭiṉai māṟṟit tāṉ tuṇintavāṟu avaṟkum aṟivuṟuttal. itu maṟutalai citaittaluṭaimaiyiṉ vāḷātu taṉkōṭ kūṟaliṉ aṭaṅkātāyiṟṟu.
" mūvaḷa picaitta lōreḻut tiṉṟē"	(tol. eḻut. 5)
eṉavum,
" nīṭṭam vēṇṭi ṉavvaḷa puṭaiya"	(tol. eḻut. 6)
eṉavum,
" kuṉṟicai moḻivayi ṉiṉṟicai niṟaikkum" 	(tol. eḻut. 41)
eṉavum mūṉṟu māttiraiyāṉ ōreḻuttu uṇṭeṉpārai vilakki vaḻakkiyalāṉ illaiyeṉṟu taṉṟuṇipu uraittavāṟu. uyirmey vēṟeḻuttaṉṟeṉpāṉ,
" puḷḷi yillā vellā meyyu
uruvuru vāki yakaramō ṭuyirttalum"	(tol. eḻut. 17)
eṉavum,
"meyyiṉ vaḻiya tuyirtōṉṟu nilaiyē"	(tol. eḻut. 18)
eṉavum vēṟupaṭātu kūṟutalum atu.
vaḻakkiṉuḷ aṟiyātārmāṭṭu vēṟupōl icaippaṉavāyiṉum aṉṟeṉṟu kūṭṭamuṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ,
" moḻippaṭut ticaippiṉun terintuvē ṟicaippiṉum
eḻuttiya ṟiriyā"	(tol. eḻut. 53)
eṉṟalum iṉameṉa ataṉpāṟ cārtti uṇarappaṭum. piṟavum aṉṉa.
(25) piṟaṉkōṭkūṟal:taṉṉūlē paṟṟākap piṟanūṟku varuvatōr ilakkaṇaṅ koḷḷumāṟu kūṟutal ; atu,
"araiyaḷapu kuṟukaṉ makara muṭaittē
icaiyiṭa ṉarukun teriyuṅ kālai"	(tol. eḻut. 13)
eṉavum,
"aḷapiṟan tuyirttalu moṟṟicai nīṭalum
. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . 
narampiṉ maṟaiya veṉmaṉār pulavar"	(tol. eḻut. 33)
eṉavum,
" paṇṇait tōṉṟiya veṇṇāṉku poruḷuṅ
kaṇṇiya puṟaṉē nāṉāṉ keṉpa"	(tol. meyp. 1)
eṉavum, ivai avvan nūluṭ koḷḷumāṟṟāṉ amaiyumeṉṟavā ṟāyiṉa.
" aḷapiṟ kōṭa lantaṇar maṟaittu"	(tol. eḻut. 102)
eṉpatu ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum; eṉṉai? avar matampaṟṟi ivaṉ koḷvatoru payaṉiṉṟākaliṉeṉpatu.
(26) aṟiyātuṭampaṭal:tāṉōtiya ilakkaṇattiṉ vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉa tāṉ aṟintilaṉākak kūṟi ataṉpuṟattuc ceyvatoru puṟaṉaṭai; avai,
" kiḷanta valla vēṟupiṟa tōṉṟiṉuṅ
kiḷantavaṟ ṟiyalā ṉuṇarntaṉar koḷalē"	(tol. col. 119)
eṉavum,
" varuva vuḷaveṉiṉum vantavaṟ ṟiyalāṉ
tiriviṉṟi muṭitta ṟeḷḷiyōr kaṭaṉē"	(tol. cey. 243)
eṉavum varum.
iṟantatu kāttalōṭu itaṉiṭai vēṟṟumaiyeṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, iṟantateṉpatu tāṉ tuṇintu collappaṭṭa poruḷākal vēṇṭum. iḵtu aṉṉataṉṟic collappaṭāta poruṇmēṟṟāki atuvun tāṉ tuṇiyappaṭāta poruḷākit tāṉ nūlceyta kālattē uḷḷavaṟṟuḷ oḻiyappōyiṉa uḷavāyiṉuṅ koḷka veṉpāṉ, 'vēṟupiṟa tōṉṟi ṉum' eṉavum, 'varupavuḷaveṉiṉum' eṉavun tēṟātu ataṉ aiyappāṭu tōṉṟac collutaliṉ itu vēṟeṉka. muḻutuṇarntāṟkallatu paḻutaṟac collalākāmaiyiṉ aḵtu avaiyaṭakkiyal pōlvatōr uttiyeṉakkoḷka.
" kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉuru iraṭṭalum
aṟiyat tōṉṟiya neṟiyiya leṉpa"	(tol. eḻut. 160)
eṉavum,
" celvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa"	(tol. eḻut. 312)
eṉavum varuvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉa vuṇarka.
(27) poruḷiṭaiyiṭutal:vēṟṟumaip poruḷiṉaic colkiṉṟa poruṇmaikkiṭaiyē peytu collutalum, colkiṉṟa poruṭku iyaipuṭaiya taṉai āṇṭuc collātu iṭaiyiṭṭupōyp piṟitoruvaḻic collutalum pōlvaṉa; avai,
" muṟaippeyark kiḷavi yēyoṭu varumē"	(tol. col. 138)
eṉṟāṟpōlac colkiṉṟa uyartiṇaip peyariṭai viravup peyar peyturaittalum, vaḻuvamaikkiṉṟa kiḷaviyākkattuḷ ōtātu eccaviyaluṭ pōkki,
" avaiyal kiḷavi maṟaittaṉar kiḷattal"	(tol. col. 443)
eṉac collutalum pōlvaṉa.
"tāṉeṉ peyaruñ cuṭṭumutaṟ peyarum
yāṉeṉ peyarum viṉāviṉ peyaru
maṉṟi yaṉaittum viḷikō ḷilavē"	(tol. col. 139)
eṉṟavaḻit, tāṉeṉṉum viravup peyariṉai iṭaippeytu vilakkutalum, vēṟṟumaik kiḷaviyōttiṉuṭ kūṟātu eñci niṉṟa vēṟṟumait tokai mutaliya tokaikaḷai eccaviyaluṭ collutalum pōlvaṉavum iṉam eṉa ataṉpāṟ cārtti yuṇarka.
(28) etirporuḷuṇarttaltāṉ kūṟiya ilakkaṇattiṟ cila piṟkālattut tiripupaṭiṉum paṭumeṉpatu, mutaṟkāla mutaṉūluṅ koṇṭuṇarnta āciriyaṉ, etirkālattu varuvatu nōkki ataṟkēṟṟatōr ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟippōtal :
" makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varainta
ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi yoṉpaḵ teṉpa
pukaraṟak kiḷanta vaḵṟiṇai mēṉa"	(tol. eḻut. 82)
eṉṟu ōtiya ilakkaṇattuc cila piṟkālattuk kuṟaiya varutal etirporu ḷeṉappaṭum. ataṉait tāṉ uṇarntu,
" kaṭico lillaik kālattup paṭiṉē"	(tol. col. 453)
eṉṟu kūṟavē muṟkūṟiya poruḷiṉai vaṟpuṟuttalām atuveṉpatu. iṅṅaṉan tiripupaṭutal aṟintē kūṟutalāṉum, etirporuḷākalāṉum, iḵtaṟiyātu uṭampaṭutaliṉaṭaṅkā tāyiṟṟu.
" poyyum vaḻuvun tōṉṟiya piṉṉar
aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam"	(tol. kaṟ. 4)
eṉpatūum ataṉpāṟ paṭum.
(29) colliṉeccañ colliyāṅ kuṇarttalcolli ṉāṟṟalāṟ peṟappaṭum poruḷiṉaiyum eṭuttōtiyāṅkuk koḷḷa vaittal; aḵtu,
" eñciya vellā meñcuta lila"	(tol. eḻut. 77)
eṉpuḻi, ellā meṉpataṉai eccappaṭuttu ataṟkākātaṉa irupattāṟu koṇṭavaḻiyum ataṉai eṭuttōtiṟ ciṟappiṉṟeṉṟu koḷḷaṟka eṉpatu itaṉ karuttu.
eccaviyaluṭ kūṟiya poruḷ akattōttuk kūṟiya poruḷōṭu oppak kūṟutalum iṉameṉa ataṉpāṟpaṭum.
'moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟa vavvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉai muṭṭiṉṟu muṭitta'lōṭu itaṉiṭai vēṟṟumaiyeṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, aḵtu eṭuttōtappaṭṭa poruṭkaṇṇateṉavum, iḵtu eṭuttōtti ṉōṭu oppa eccappaṭa vaittukkoḷḷum ilakkaṇam eṉavum ataṉō ṭitaṉiṭai vēṟṟumaiyuṇarka.
(30) tantu puṇarnturaittal: uḷporu ḷallataṉai uḷatu pōlat tantu kūṭa uṇarttal ; avai,
" aḷapeṭai yacainilai yākalu murittē"	(tol. cey. 16)
eṉavum,
" kuṟiliṇai yukara malvaḻi yāṉa"	(tol. cey. 4)
eṉavuṅ kūṟuṅkāl aḷapeṭaiyiṉai eḻuttup pōlavuṅ kuṟṟukarattiṉaik kuṟṟeḻuttup pōlavum ōtutal pōlvaṉa.
" meyyī ṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal"	(tol. eḻut. 104)
eṉavum,
" meyyuyir nīṅkiṟ ṟaṉṉuru vākum "	(tol. eḻut. 139)
eṉavum mēṟkūṟiya puḷḷiyiṉaiyē orupayaṉōkki mīṭṭum puḷḷi peṟumeṉak kūṟutal ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
" kuṟumaiyu neṭumaiyu maḷaviṟ kōṭaliṟ
ṟoṭarmoḻi yellā neṭṭeḻut tiyala"	(tol. eḻut. 50)
eṉpatēpaṟṟi,
" neṭiyataṉ muṉṉa roṟṟumey keṭutalum"	(tol. eḻut. 160)
eṉpuḻit toṭarmoḻiyai neṭṭeḻuttākkik kōṭalum atu.
(31) ñāpakaṅkūṟalcūttirañ ceyyuṅkāl ataṟku ōtiya ilakkaṇa vakaiyāṉē cilvakaiyeḻuttiṉ ceyyuṭṭākavum nāṭutaliṉṟip poruṇaṉi viḷaṅkavuñ ceyyātu aritum peritumāka nalintu ceytu maṟṟum ataṉāṉē vēṟupala poruḷuṇarttal : atu,
" meypeṟu marapiṟ ṟoṭaivakai tāmē
. . . uṇarntici ṉōrē"	(tol. cey. 101)
eṉa varutal. itaṉāṟkoṇṭa poruṇmaiyellāñ ceyyuḷiyaluṭ kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
" eppeyar muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi
yakki ṉiṟutimeym micaiyoṭuṅ keṭumē"	(tol. eḻut. 128)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. piṟavum aṉṉa.
(32) uyttukkoṇṭuṇartaloruvaḻi oru poruḷ col liyakkāl ataṉkaṇṇē maṟṟoru poruḷiṉaiyuṅ koṇarntu koṇṭaṟiyumāṟu tōṉṟac ceytal : avai,
" neṭṭeḻut timparun toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟuṅ
kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntē"	(tol. eḻut. 36)
eṉa iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum kūṟi, ataṉāṉ īṟu ākkaṅkōṭaluṅ,
" kuṟṟiya lukara muṟaippeyar maruṅki
ṉoṟṟiya nakaramicai nakaramoṭu mutalum"	(tol. eḻut. 67)
eṉa mutalākkaṅ kūṟi, ataṉāṉē iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum atuvēyeṉak kōṭalum pōlvaṉa.
peyarviṉaikku ōtiya ilakkaṇam oḻinta coṟkuñ cevvaṉañ cellumeṉṟu kōṭal ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum; piṟavum aṉṉa.
maṟṟu nutaliyataṟitalōṭu itaṉiṭai vēṟṟumaiyeṉṉai yeṉiṉ,- atu vāḷātu payamila kūṟiyatu pōlak kūṟiyavaḻi ivvāṟu kūṟiyatu iṉṉa karuttup pōlumeṉṟu aṟiya vaittalum, uraivakai yāṉum nutaliyataṟiyac collutalumām; iḵtu aṉṉataṉṟi, accūttiran taṉṉāṉ oruporuḷ payantataṉṟalaiyum piṉṉoruporuḷ peṟa varutaliṉ itu vēṟeṉpatu.
maṟṟu ñāpakaṅ kūṟalōṭu itaṉiṭai vēṟṟumai yeṉṉai yeṉiṉ, - payamillatu pōlavum aritum peritumākavum iyaṟṟi eḷituñ ciṟitumāka iyaṟṟātu cūttirañ ceytal vēṟupāṭē nimitta mākat tōṟṟik koḷvatoru poruḷpeṟa vaittaliṉ ituvum vēṟeṉappaṭumeṉpatu.
meyppaṭa nāṭic colliya alla piṟa avaṇ variṉum - uyttuk koṇṭuṇartaloṭu mēṟkūṟiya muppattiraṇṭum iccūttirattuḷ eṭuttōtiya poruḷvakaiyāṉ ārāyntu collappaṭṭaṉavaṉṟē? aṅṅaṉañ collātaṉa piṟavum innūluḷ variṉum; colliya vakaiyāṟ curuṅka nāṭi - uttivakaiyeṉa vakuttukkoṇṭu ōtiya muppattiraṇṭu pakutiyāṉ aṭaṅkumāṟu ārāyntu; maṉattiṉ eṇṇi mācaṟat terintu koṇṭu- ōtappaṭṭa utti palavum oruṅku variṉum uḷḷattāṉ teḷḷitiṉ ārāyntu mayakkan tīra vēṟu vēṟu terintu vāṅkik koṇṭu;  iṉattil cērtti uṇarttal vēṇṭum- muppattiraṇṭākum ēṟṟa vakaiyāṉ iṉañ cārtti maṟṟavaṟṟai iṉṉatituveṉap peyar kūṟalvēṇṭum, aṅṅaṉan tokaniṉṟa vaḻiyum vēṟuvēṟu koṇṭu; nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūlē - talaimai cāṉṟa āciriyarāṟ kūṟappaṭṭa nūl e-ṟu.
eṇṇiya muppattiraṇṭu mallaṉa tōṉṟiṉum avaṟṟuḷ aṭakki, avaitām oruṅku variṉum vēṟu terintu iṉantōṟuñ cērttutalai avāvi niṟkum īṇṭu ōtiyanūleṉpatu karuttu.
colliya alla piṟa avaṇ varumāṟum, avai colliyavakai yāṟ curuṅka nāṭi iṉattiṟ cērttumāṟum mēṟ kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
iṉi, oruṅku palavutti vantavaḻi uḷḷattāṉ teḷḷiti ṉeṇṇit terintu koṇṭu iṉattiṟ cērttutal varumāṟu :
" aṉṉa piṟavuṅ kiḷanta valla
paṉmuṟai yāṉum parantaṉa varūum
uricco lellām poruṭkuṟai kūṭṭa
iyaṉṟa maruṅki ṉiṉaitteṉa vaṟiyum
varamputamak kiṉmaiyiṉ vaḻinaṉi kaṭaippiṭittu
ōmpaṭai yāṇaiyiṟ kiḷantavaṟ ṟiyalāṟ
pāṅkuṟa vuṇarta leṉmaṉār pulavar"	(tol. col. 396)
eṉṟavaḻi,
" aṉṉa piṟavuṅ kiḷanta valla
paṉmuṟai yāṉum parantaṉa varūu 
muriccol"
eṉpatu, iṟantatu kāttalām; eṉṉai? icaiyuṅ kuṟippum paṇpumē yaṉṟic cīrttiyum puṉiṟum pōlvaṉa vēṟum uḷa eṭuttōtap paṭṭaṉa eṉavum, eṭuttōtātu icaiyuṅ kuṟippum paṇpumaṉṟic cēṇeṉṟun toṟuveṉṟum varuvaṉa uḷaveṉavuṅ kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ,
" varamputamak kiṉmaiyiṉ"
eṉpatu etirporu ḷuṇarttalum aṟiyātuṭaṉpaṭalumām.
" ōmpaṭai yāṇai"
eṉpatu āṇai kūṟalām. iṅṅaṉam oru cūttirattuṭ pala vantavaḻi,  oṉṟē uttiyeṉṟuṇarātu, maṉattiṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇṭu ivvāṟṟāṉ iṉattiṟ cērttuka eṉṟāṉ āciriyaṉeṉpatu.
" marapunilai tiriyā māṭciya vāki
yuraipaṭu nūṟā miruvakai iyala"	(tol. mara. 93)
eṉpatu mutalāka ittuṇaiyum vaḻakkunūlilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṉāṉ, eḻunilatteḻunta ceyyuḷiṉ (tol. cey. 476) ituvum oṉṟākaliṉ itaṉaic ceyyuḷiyaliṟ kūṟiṉāṉ. īṇṭu marapu kūṟumvaḻi oḻinta ceyyuṭkup pōlātu, nūliṟkup poruṭpaṭaiyākiya yāppuk kuṟṟaṅka ḷum uttivakaiyuṅ kūṟal vēṇṭutaliṟ kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟākkāṟ ' pāṭṭiṉ marapu' kaṭṭurai pōlavuṅ,
"kaṭṭaḷai araṅkiṉṟi vaṭṭā ṭiyatu" 
pōlavum, varampiṉṟi vēṇṭiyavāṟu nūlceytal vilakkiṉṟāvāṉ cellum ; cellavē, eḻucīrāṉākiya muṭukiyalaṭiyāṉum ettuṇaiyum nīṇṭatoru vañcippāṭṭāṉum piṟavum vēṇṭiyavāṟuñ cūttirañ ceytalum, oruvaṉ peyariṉai avvac cūttirac ceyyuḷuṭ cārttu vakaiyāṟ peytu kūṟalum, uṇarttappaṭum poruḷiṉai mutaṉūluṭ kiṭantavāṟu pōlātu marapunilai tiriyac ceytalum, nālvakai yāppoṭu māṟupaṭac ceytalum, vēṇṭiyavar vēṇṭiyavāṟṟāṟ cila poruḷkaḷai vēṟu tōṟṟikkoṇṭu nūlceytalum vilakkiṉṟākal paṭumeṉpatu.
iṉi uttivakaiyum avvāṟē iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉa veṉappaṭum. eṉṉai? uṇarttappaṭum poruḷ ituveṉṟu aṟivittalum, eḻuttuc coṟporuḷeṉap pakuttuk koṇṭu atikārañ ceytalum, uṇarvu pulaṅkoḷḷumāṟṟāṉ tokuttuk kāṭṭalum, maṟṟu avaṟṟai vakuttuk kāṭṭiyavaḻip payamila kūṟaleṉṟu karutāmal atutaṉṉā ṉoru payampaṭac ceytalum, mutaṉūlāyiṉa vellām nūṟporu ḷuṇartaṟkuk karuviyākaluñ cūttiraccurukkattuk kētuvākalum uṭaimaiyiṉ avaiyum vēṇṭappaṭṭaṉa veṉpatu.
iṉi, avaṟṟai ittuṇaiyeṉa varaiyaṟākkāl, ettuṇaiyum palavāki ikantōṭutalum, vaḻinūṉ mutaṉūl vaḻittaṉṟākalum paṭum; muppattumūṉṟeḻut teṉṟāṉāyiṉum, appeṟṟittaṉṟi aṟupattā ṟākak koḷḷavaittāṉeṉṟu utti kūṟalum uṭampaṭuvāṉātal cellumeṉa maṟukka.
iṉi,
" nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūl"
eṉṟataṉāṉē, pāyiracceyyuḷuñ cūttiracceyyuḷum āciriyappāvum veṇpāvum peṟṟu varutalum, avvirupāvum peṟṟu varutaṟkaṇ, pāṭṭuppōla  ellāvuṟuppum peṟutaṟkuc cellā veṉpatūum, māttirai mutalākap pāvīṟāka vanta patiṉoṉṟum vaṇṇaṅkaḷuḷ ēṟpaṉa koḷḷiṉuṅ koḷḷumeṉavum, yāppuṟuppuk koḷḷuṅkāl īṇṭōtiya marapuṅ koḷḷappaṭumeṉavuṅ koḷka. uraikkuṅ kāṇṭikaikkum ivaṟṟuḷḷum ēṟpaṉa aṟintu koḷka.
iṉṉum ' nuṉittaku pulavar' eṉṟataṉāṉē, tantiramuñ cūttiramum viruttiyumeṉa mūṉṟum oruvarēyaṉṟi oṉṟu oruvar ceytalum, iraṇṭu ceytalum peṟappaṭumeṉṟalum, orucālai māṇākkarun tammāciriyarun tammiṭai nūl kēṭṭa māṇākkarum pāyirañ ceyyappeṟupa veṉṟalum, potuppāyiramuñ ciṟappuppāyiramumeṉa appāyirantām iraṇṭāmeṉṟalum, īvōṉṟaṉmaiyum ītaliyaṟkai yuṅ koḷvōṉṟaṉmaiyuṅ kōṭaṉmarapumeṉṟa nāṉkuṟuppuṭaiyatu potup pāyirameṉṟalum, ataṉvaḻiyē kūṟappaṭuñ ciṟappup pāyirantāṉ eṭṭilakkaṇa muṭaitteṉṟalum, avai ākkiyōṉ peyarum vaḻiyum ellaiyum nūṟpeyarum yāppum nutaliya poruḷuṅ kēṭpōrum payaṉu (naṉṉūl 47) meṉappaṭumeṉṟalum, avaitām nūṟkiṉṟiyamaiyāveṉak koḷḷappaṭutaluṅ kūṟi muṭikka.
ciṟappuppāyirattāṉē nūlilakkaṇam oruvakaiyāṉ uṇarap paṭum; potuppāyirattāṉē āciriyarum māṇākkarum nūluraittalum nūlkēṭṭalum mācaṟavaṟintu urai naṭāttuvārāka. ataṉāṉē ivai nūṉmukattiṉiṉṟu nilāvumeṉpatu.	(110)
" nuṇmā ṇuḻaipula millā ṉeḻiṉalam
maṇmāṇ puṉaipāvai yaṟṟu"	(kuṟaḷ . 407)
" olkāp perumpukaḻt tolkāp piyaṉaṭi
palkāṟ paravutu meḻuttoṭu
colkā maruporuṭ ṭokaitikaḻ poruṭṭē"
marapiyal muṟṟiṟṟu. marapiyaṟkuppērāciriyarurai muṟṟiṟṟu.
